## Standing Completion Rule

When any Hermes worker completes a task, it should add:

```text
Deliverable: <absolute path or URL>
Summary: <one sentence describing what Jeff can use it for>
Next action: <what Jeff or the next worker should do>
```

If there are multiple files, the worker should update this index and comment on the task with the index path.
